1.16 BILLING / CREDITS / COMMERCE
------------------------------------------------------------
CREDIT-BASED SEATS ON AN OPENROUTER RAIL [PLANNED(K-24)] the FIRST
  commerce surface -- subscription -> monthly CREDITS (real provider $) ->
  spent per metered usage -> ELAI pays upstream -> ELAI controls limits/
  rollover/routing (the OpenRouter shape; SAFE, metered+capped, never
  "unlimited" an agent loop can detonate). ~70% the plumbing exists (J-28
  meter / J-49 seat-quota-spend / C-5+J-81 caps+runaway-kill / H-9
  $/resolved-task / B-37 backends / F-4+I-18+J-69 audit-grade journal). THE
  CREDIT LEDGER = an SSB sub-journal (balance = fold sum(Granted)-
  sum(Settled)-sum(open Reserved)-sum(Expired), NEVER a mutable counter;
  hash-chained I-18/J-69; single-writer serialization = the double-spend
  defense). SIX vertical slices L1..L6: CreditLedgerEvent + balance-fold +
  `billing balance` / the reserve-settle 8-step loop (estimate -> Reserved
  -> call w/ J-29 meter + J-81 runaway cap -> Settled(actual) release
  same-tick) / OpenRouter MANAGED backend (B-37 #12, hand-rolled per J-54,
  no new dep) + actual-cost capture booked via J-28 / J-49 admission gated
  on balance + tier rate limits + out-of-credits refusal / subscription
  refill + rollover (seats.toml DATA) + the payment-webhook -> Granted/
  Expired seam (Stripe/Paddle OUTSIDE ELAI, PCI scope zero) / the
  $/resolved-task SAFEDELTA measurement that GATES the "$20=$200" pitch
  (ELAI-measured, never asserted, KD-1/ID-5). GD-2: ELAI's SSB credit
  journal is the financial source of truth, NOT OpenRouter's/LiteLLM's
  spend store (the single biggest way to ship this wrong; LiteLLM-as-ledger
  REJECTED, see Section 4). I-51 threaded: OpenRouter is transport+meter
  NOT a dynamic model registry (the registry rejection stands). margin
  reality = ~15% eaten on the frontier portion (5.5% fee + 10% bonus), so
  J-55 routing quality IS the business model (gate the launch on the
  measured local-lane resolve-rate, not faith). PRIOR-CAUTION
  (project_market_positioning_2026): sold as "proven more value per
  dollar, verified" (TRUST), never "cheap"; first revenue plausibly the
  team/agency seat tier @ $39-49/seat (GTM memory), not consumer tiers.
  COMPOSES ssb.rs/I-18/J-69/J-28/J-29/J-49/C-5/J-81-E-39/B-37/J-54/J-55/

================================================================================
SECTION 3 — DOCTRINE FOR NEW PLANS (what binds plan AUTHORING; canon
  files named)
================================================================================
W-1..W-10 (F-0_INDEX canon): done-gate = wiring_contract_check.py
  --run-trigger PASS; default-off env gate is never a terminal state
  (W-2 == R9); reachable from a REAL production entrypoint (test-only
  caller = wire-dark); frontend_pair + cross_lang_diff for any dashboard
  signal; observable_effect = live end-to-end trigger (RunPod work ships
  a deterministic fixture harness + separately-gated live checkbox); >=1
  acceptance item through the dashboard or justified N/A; every
  integration anchor grep-confirmed at authoring (W-7);
  produces/consumes symmetry; SSB types flow the golden lock with the
  bump named from the LIVE baseline never hard-coded (W-9);
  network/subprocess/RunPod axes split off the fail-closed hot path
  (W-10).
GD-1..GD-5 (G-0_INDEX): COMPOSE-NOT-RESTATE (consume prior
  verdicts/symbols; restating a prior artifact = P0); every emitted
  number recomputable from the SSB journal under ELAI_SEED;
  gate-ON-vs-OFF ablation framing never a leaderboard rank; no gate
  flips default-ON until FP/over-block rate bounded on >=3 real runs; no
  fail-open/cloud-opaque path advertises a guarantee it does not
  deliver.
HD-1..HD-5 (H Tracks 1-2): efficiency numbers MEASURED per
  model-harness-config, never multiplied into a headline; never bust the
  byte-stable cache prefix (volatile content strictly after the last
  breakpoint; masking drops trailing, never rewrites); output bills 5x
  and decoding is sacred (never constrained-decode reasoning roles;
  grammars only on pure op-emission); canvas/model-driving gates
  auditor-driven off-agent; a rendered picture is not proof
  (coverage_count is the backstop).
TD-1..TD-5 (H Track 3): persisted agent-unresettable trial count N;
  evaluator+harness+thresholds+ledger frozen in the gate-manifest
  mutable=0 set; LLM is T3-advisory never the load-bearing gate; each
  gate kills its own frozen mutant corpus before ACTIVE; name the honest
  undecidable residue and route it to human L3.
RD-1..RD-4 (H Track 6): any optimized reward credits abstention and
  penalizes confident-wrong at t/(1-t) (abstain bit read from D-16,
  never re-derived); verifiers retained on conditional
  error-decorrelation not standalone accuracy; sound-checker-first,
  coverage is the lever; budget scales with serial depth never window
  size.
VD-1..VD-5 (H Track 7): self-improvement fitness is execution-grounded
  never bytes/self-confidence; trap detection behavioral with MEASURED
  recall on a frozen contrastive corpus; promote on
  worst-case/variance-penalized, lineage-diverse verdicts with
  PRMBench-calibrated tau; verifier must be stronger-than-generator and
  cross-family; honest residue, loop stays dark/allowlisted.
RV-1..RV-4 (H Track 8): never clobber the human (snapshot-before-restore
  fork); lossless store / lossy view (trim is a projection, never the
  record); redo is a forward edge not a destructible stack; honest
  irreversibility (external side effects surfaced from the saga log,
  never auto-reverted).
CL-1..CL-4 (H Track 9): one window source of truth per backend;
  regime-opposite policy selected by live BackendMode; unloaded content
  stays unloaded on local (selective reload only); never advertise a
  cache hit a path cannot deliver (measure cache-read per regime).
ID-1..ID-6 + IC-1..IC-18 (I-0 + I-GROUNDING-MAP, BINDING): generator
  proposes / orchestrator owns state; CoW isolation; reversible
  receipts; L1 HardVeto never overridable; ELAI-measured baselines never
  paper constants; learned artifacts promote through the ledger.
  Highest-impact corrections: no batch-racing (IC-1), deterministic
  compression only / ciphers BANNED (IC-2/3), search is a ladder not
  MCTS-first (IC-6), SFT-before-GRPO (IC-7), one calibration impl
  (IC-13), bind to the wired trust spine never restate (IC-16), personas
  dropped for executor/researcher kept for auditor/critic (IC-17),
  post-parse schema re-validation (IC-18).
JD-1..JD-4 (J-0): harness (.claude/) legs have no SSB — off-agent
  evidence = hook-log/state.json rows, never recollection; hooks
  non-blocking by construction; pre_tool_check.py is never edited.
AUTHORING MECHANICS: vertical-slice PRE_GATE (producer + in-plan
  consumer + smoke trigger in the SAME unit; bare "add fn X" REJECTED at
  decomposition); new SSB action_type = Pattern-2
  single-variant-with-Kind discriminator where it applies + full 9-tuple
  golden-lock lockstep + TOTAL_VARIANTS bump (a partial add breaks the
  build); any new default-OFF flag needs a wire-dark-allowlist.toml row
  (type/owner/expiry/promotion_plan_id/smoke) or it FAILS R9; every
  arXiv id/citation WebSearch-verified before it enters a plan (2 bad
  cites each found in B-40/B-42/B-41 eras); per-plan grounding map
  (_grounding/*-GROUNDING-MAP.md) with live grep evidence is the W-7
  norm for new letters; verify with cargo check --workspace (per-crate
  -p cache can report FALSE-GREEN) + the action_type_lock golden test.

TIME-AWARENESS VERDICTS (2026-06-26, the user's time-awareness idea — conversational
  timestamps + agent/tool-time + decouple-so-it-cant-get-stuck; full file =
  workflow/reasoning/time-awareness-design-2026-06-25.md, spec-eval AS-SPECIFIED, code-
  grounded; K-native half = K-14, technical half = a J time track; do not re-litigate):
- META: the idea is correct + necessary (the temporally-blind failure is MEASURED,
  arXiv:2510.23853 / 2601.13206) but conflates TWO planes — TIME-AS-CONTEXT (the prompt
  stamp, judgment) vs TIME-AS-CONTROL (a supervisor watchdog + decoupling, a guarantee);
  keeping them separate is the whole design (letting an injected timestamp drive a timeout
  breaks determinism AND makes the guarantee model-dependent). Part B is ~80% already wired
  (background_task_id/BackgroundTaskManager/WatchdogTimeout/AgentNotificationEmitted G3-G6 +
  I-1 supervisor) — a WIRING/EXPOSURE plan, not a from-scratch build.
- NEW (K-native, DISCIPLINE+PACKAGE): K-14 = the time-determinism doctrine TZ-1..TZ-4
  (volatile-slot-after-the-cache-breakpoint / OsClock-journaled-as-input / never-a-gate-
  input ND-1 / supervisor-plane-liveness-outside-the-replay-set; the R-1 cache+replay-
  regression pre-empt) as a binding pre-gate, + banking the replayable-auditable-async-
  tools differentiator (IMP-9) as a tagged claim. The differentiator is GENUINE on-wedge
  whitespace: nobody else lands the async-tool lifecycle in a deterministic replayable
  taint-tagged journal (G18 SOTA harnesses return a task_id, none replays it).

  two-plane J time track, all BOUND by K-14's TZ-1..TZ-4): J-99 TIME-AS-CONTEXT plane =

  overthinking guard, J-85). The single highest-leverage move = name ONE typed reasoning-state carrier
  (J-83) the pieces compose around. The DECISIVE CORRECTION (the deepest grounding, 3 independent 2024-2026
  papers — Reflexion self-correction blind spot + ToT self-eval-is-the-weak-link + Lanham faithfulness): a
  model cannot reliably ground itself or judge which of its own parts is "sure", so "the part the model is
  sure about proceeds" MUST mean "the part an EXTERNAL RECEIPT confirms proceeds" = the shipped trust spine

  GAP-2 keyword-grep trap). So the reuse-fitness MUST be off-agent + execution-grounded + cross-family +
  abstention-aware (NOT "it was retrieved", NOT LLM-judged-helpful) and reuse-into-generation is observe-first +
  never-into-the-auditor. K-19 makes this a binding rule before the capability is built.

  reasoning (measured Qwen3-4B 0->100 on MRCRv2) = ELAI's OWN harness>model thesis, measured, landing on ELAI's local small-model
  lane. The rubric G6 gate (WHO chooses the decomposition: full-RLM = the LLM at runtime; ELAI = plan-first/FSM/TD-3) makes ELAI
  DELIBERATELY Level-2/3 — a CHOICE trading model-chosen flexibility for determinism + off-agent verification (the trust spine),
  NOT a deficiency. "Be more RLM at the orchestration grain" is an ANTI-GOAL.
- THE MEASURED DESIGN TENSION RESOLVED (the F4 open question): the RLM scaffold works by the model writing Python to slice/recurse
  over a context variable; J-55 forbids general CodeAct on the sub-14B local lane. ELAI's two experiments measured the resolution:
  a CONSTRAINED named-op/slice path sits BELOW the general code-mode collapse threshold (named-ops held 100% on a weak 8B lane)
  while FREE-code RLM is erratic AND fabricates (hallucinated "42" on 7/8 hard multi-hop tasks at 8xW, recursion never engaged even
  on a 120B). And the path to ~100% on multi-hop is DETERMINISM, not model-navigation (ORACLE deterministic traversal 8/8;
  model-driven nav PLATEAUS ~25%; a deterministic graph-hop hybrid made a weak 8B hit 87%).

SECTION 5 — EXTENSION POINTS (the canonical ways a new feature attaches)
================================================================================
NEW SSB ACTION_TYPE: Pattern-2 single-variant with a *Kind discriminator
  enum (43+ applications); full 9-tuple lockstep: SsbActionType variant
  + SsbPayload arm + payload struct + all_test_payloads() entry +
  action_type_lock.golden row + TOTAL_VARIANTS bump + SCHEMA_VERSION
  bump + match arms + ts-rs binding where surfaced; bump from the LIVE
  integers (ssb.rs:3064 / action_type_lock.rs:662), never a doc number;
  a partial add breaks the workspace (91-error incident).

  agent_substate for AUDITING/MEMORIZING); transitions additive-only;
  gate via InteractiveGate/AutonomousGate EvidenceCheck.
CLI SUBCOMMAND: crates/cli slash-command dispatch (args.rs + repl.rs +
  *_cmd.rs module, e.g. prebind_cmd.rs); pair every new verb with its
  FSM gate; CLI is a Tier-C subprocess context.
DASHBOARD SURFACE: window or drawer Svelte 5 runes component +
  #[tauri::command] in src-tauri (commands_*.rs precedent) +
  generate_handler! registration + ts-rs bindings pair; W-4:
  frontend_pair + cross_lang_diff.py PASS; engine crates stay OUT of
  src-tauri Cargo.toml (adding any = user confirmation); verify via
  dashboard-smoker.
SIDECAR ENDPOINT: python/elai_inference/server.py route + Rust client
  pair in providers (runtime_client.rs); cross_lang_diff.py flags a
  missing pair; sidecar runs REMOTE only; the live client uses the
  streaming path (PrefixKVCache bypass caveat).
FEATURE FLAG: ELAI_*_ENABLE or cargo feature; MUST land a
  wire-dark-allowlist.toml row (type kill_switch|experiment, owner,
  expiry_date, promotion_plan_id, smoke test exercising the ON path) or
  R9 FAILS; 79 active rows; gate fileset is R8-frozen
  (gate_freeze_check.py --lock after human review).
CONTRACTS TEST FAMILY: crates/contracts/tests/<id>_*_contract.rs
  (precedent h50/h51/h52/h53); NOTE crates/orchestrator/tests/
  integration tests do NOT link (ort/ndarray rlib) — put smokes as
  in-crate #[cfg(test)] lib tests; tracing-test asserts must wrap
